Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The lie_groups variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 102 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 881 and intersection form of rank 4.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the lie_groups decomposition. By minimality, the element a^102 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 881-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
